## [0] correct=True · `HNBA-2017-18-Confidentiality-Agreement.txt`

- request_id: `eabc3110ee93`
- error_type: `none`
- latency_ms: 12806.59

### Query

Consider HNBA's Confidentiality Agreement; Does the document restrict the use of Confidential Information to the purposes stated in the Agreement?

### Gold

1. The Board Member agrees that the Confidential Information he or she receives shall be used solely for the benefit of the HNBA Board Work, and that all rights to the proprietary and novel features contained in the Confidential Information are reserved by the disclosing Party. The Board Member will not use or disclose Confidential Information to any third party for any purpose other than the performance of his or her duties as a Board Member of the HNBA unless and until the HNBA expressly authorizes the disclosure in writing.

### RAG Answer

Yes. The HNBA Confidentiality Agreement restricts the Board Member’s use of Confidential Information to the purposes stated in the Agreement.

Specifically, it provides that Confidential Information received by the Board Member “shall be used solely for the benefit of the HNBA Board Work,” and the Board Member may not use or disclose it for any purpose other than performing his or her duties as an HNBA Board Member unless the HNBA expressly authorizes the disclosure in writing.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标中关于使用目的限制的核心要点，虽未提及披露方保留权利，但不影响对问题的命中。
- gap: 无

## [1] correct=True · `IBC-PMS-NDA-agreement.txt`

- request_id: `18586bcf4cbe`
- error_type: `none`
- latency_ms: 18914.64

### Query

Consider the Non-Disclosure Agreement between IBC and PMS; Does the document mention whether or not the Receiving Party is allowed to reverse engineer any objects which embody the Disclosing Party's Confidential Information?

### Gold

(a) Vendor shall:
(v) Refrain from reverse engineering, decompiling or disassembling any software code disclosed by IBC to Vendor under the terms of this Agreement, except as expressly permitted by applicable law.

### RAG Answer

Yes. The document addresses reverse engineering and generally prohibits it.

Under the Agreement, the Vendor (the Receiving Party) must “refrain from reverse engineering, decompiling or disassembling any software code disclosed by IBC,” except as expressly permitted by applicable law.

### Query

Consider the Non-Disclosure Agreement between Dollar General and Portfolio Entity; Does the document indicate that the Agreement does not grant the Receiving Party any rights to the Confidential Information?

As part of Buyer’s agreement to accept and use the Confidential Materials and Audit as such use is defined herein, and as consideration for the release of the Confidential Materials by Owner, BUYER HEREBY WAIVES ANY AND ALL ACTUAL OR POTENTIAL RIGHTS, CLAIMS, CAUSES OF ACTION OR DEMAND BUYER MIGHT HAVE REGARDING ANY FORM OF WARRANTY, EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, OF ANY KIND OR TYPE, RELATING TO THE CONFIDENTIAL MATERIALS.

No — not expressly.

Based on the provided NDA fragments, the document does **not contain an explicit clause stating that the Agreement grants the Receiving Party no rights to the Confidential Information**. - **Section 3** also permits disclosure to Buyer’s Representatives who need to know the information for evaluating the transaction, subject to confidentiality.
- **Section 5** says the Confidential Materials are provided “solely as a courtesy” and that the Buyer waives rights/claims relating to warranties concerning the Confidential Materials. However, that is a waiver of warranty-related claims, not an express statement that the Buyer has no rights to the Confidential Information itself.

So similar information that is actually derived from the confidential Evaluation Material would remain subject to confidentiality restrictions.

**Bottom line:** Yes, the agreement permits independent development and use of residual general knowledge, but it does not permit the Buyer to use or disclose information that is derived from or incorporates the Company’s Evaluation Material.

4. was already in Buyer’s possession before disclosure, from a source other than the Company or its Representatives, provided that source is not bound by confidentiality to the Company.

So, the Buyer may acquire similar information from a third party **if it comes from a non-confidential third-party source not bound by confidentiality to the Company**, or otherwise falls within one of the exclusions. If the third party is bound by confidentiality to the Company, or the information is otherwise Evaluation Material, the confidentiality restrictions would still apply.

### Judge

- reason: 系统回答覆盖了金标的关键要点，即接收方可以从不受保密义务约束的第三方来源获取类似信息；虽然回答有扩展说明，但不影响命中。
- gap: 无
